# Chapter 7-b — Group E: C-7O

**Document:** `NH_MASTER-21_SYSTEM_BEHAVIOR_v0_1_CANDIDATE__CH07-b.md`  
**Status:** CANDIDATE  
**Source repository:** `nesgeva/NH-GOVERNANCE`  
**Source commit:** `6a7160ba688ba4e433a31899162815df7e2bab17`  

This piece covers the two result-input kinds, seven-field proposed connection, Ness's four response options, causation limits, three separate objects and meanings, conditional downstream uses, detection controls, six result states, accepted result-record structures and result-return operation/logging boundaries. It reuses the delivered stage/level, presentation, operation, grounding and lifecycle owners. CH07-c retains full authority, preparation, authorization, recurring scope, execution, violation/correction and emergency-stop mechanics; CH08 retains full privacy/relevance/LMAC and BOP/OOP/affirmation mechanisms; CH09 retains identity/output machinery; CH10-e visual presentation; CH11 complete side-path assembly; CH12 register regeneration. Exact detection, confirmation workflow, finer result classification, downstream triggers and B-CYCLE-5 composition remain open.

Citation keys: V10 = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_MASTER-20_CORRECTED_v10.md`; MAP = `02_WORKING_MAP/NH_COMPLETE_DESIGN_AND_WIRING_MAP_v1_6_CANDIDATE.md`; DD = `01_AUTHORITATIVE/NH_DECISION_DEFAULTS-S19_v2_2.md`; `04/` = `04_ACCEPTED_STANDALONE_DESIGNS/`; `05/` = `05_ACTIVE_CANDIDATE/`; NHD identifiers locate decisions in the pinned index and do not replace behavior citations.

### C-7O — Action-Result Return Path (§7O)
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The conceptually designed, not built, return path for action outcomes, without direct system access to reality. [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — An earlier action, Ness's reported result or incoming material possibly related to the action, and any separate Ness response. [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]
- Does: DESIGNED — Preserves two result types, proposes detected connections, keeps action/result/connection separate and records the six result states without inventing causation or Ness's judgment. [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — Reported result material, a possible-result proposal, response events and honestly qualified result assessments. [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Silently declare detected material the action's result, infer causation from timing/similarity, merge the three objects, auto-retry, or rewrite an action to remove a dispute. [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]
- Fails closed by: DESIGNED — Unknown outcomes remain unknown; disputed links remain recorded as rejected connection events beside preserved action and result objects. [V10 §7O] [MAP C-7O]

## Scope, paths and source dispositions

The complete V10 result-return section is placed in source order: two result types, seven proposal fields, four Ness response options, causation limits, three linked objects, three separate kinds of meaning, possible downstream uses, detection controls and six result states. Each success/partial/failure state retains its confirmed-versus-revisable interpretation branches. Cancellation retains trigger, reason, cancellation-time action state and separately completed versus intended effects. Unknown stays unknown with active uncertainty-marked state/open-loop context; elapsed time supplies no result. A disputed link produces rejected-connection history beside unchanged endpoints and may lead only to another explicit proposal.

Accepted Bundle 4 adds record structure and operational protections, not a completed detection/confirmation/classification algorithm. The three-name confirmed/rejected/disputed event summary does not cancel V10 modification or indefinite unresolved status, so no source conflict is invented from that summary. Shared action-family stage/level fields and all B27 wording stay with C-7N.7/.8. The stable Action ID is first placed at C-7O.2.1 and is reusable by CH07-c; it is not a possibility ID, operation ID, kernel ID or result-root ID. B8 records consume the already placed common record-operation, B9, evidence-family, log and lifecycle atoms without renaming them. Technical recovery of a failed record operation is distinct from retrying an action because its result was negative or unknown.

The normal source front door remains the only root route. Bundle 6 observation material is merely another properly ingested source, not direct result confirmation, and absence of correction is never confirmation. Full BOP/OOP schemas, capture windows and reaction mechanics remain CH08-d/e/f. B15's output_type action_result is a TSC output-schema label, not a replacement for the six §7O states or a new intake path. The formal Bundle 2 source-inventory match supplies no new result-return relevance declaration. Future unified search may address action/result history but remains later interface/search scope.

The accepted durable kernel retains component-owned IDs, claims, decisions and terminal truth, and expressly leaves all B-CYCLE composition open. Its generic coordination cannot confirm a result, replace actual action/root/connection identities or authorize execution; only that result-owned boundary is placed here. General kernel mechanics and its deliberately unassigned controlled ID remain outside this piece. The earlier framework addition is read as scope direction, not as a substitute for the later accepted mechanical package.

The incoming C-7N, C-7N.4, C-7N.5.1, C-7N.9, C-7D and C-7D.14.2.4 uses are reciprocated without changing prior files. Shared C-7N.7/.8 incoming uses are consumed at their existing ACCEPTED status. C-24.14 is conditional on using an accepted connection and does not override result-specific confirmation or causal limits. Exact downstream processes not named by a source remain a bounded allowance, with no invented owner or path ID.

Discovery searched part ID, action-result/return terminology, possible results and disputed-linkage language across accepted and active files, including decision records. The four active indices retain full NHD-M7O/NHD-BU4 navigation. Recovery-ledger FR-0214/0215/0324/0469–0471 are Appendix B tracking only; unrelated historical endpoint/TSC schema matches are not imported. No archive body was opened. No new earlier-piece defect or source conflict was established. All prior conflicts and defects, including CH06-d grouped USED BY rows and the CH07-a gentle-question conflict, remain unchanged.

Full authority/preparation/authorization/execution/violation/correction/emergency-stop mechanics remain CH07-c; privacy/relevance/LMAC and observation/affirmation mechanisms CH08; identity/output machinery CH09; visual interface CH10-e; CY-E and other side-path assembly CH11; regenerated registers CH12. Complete B-CYCLE-5 operation identity, cross-stage transactions and full-cycle recovery remain open.

## Plain-gate and empty-box review

Every card was reviewed for misplaced restrictions, failures and gates. Actual Ness-response branches, result confirmations, intake, downstream owner conditions, connection current-use, privacy/authority and crash-case predicates are named at the consuming card. Pure record fields, source-defined values, prohibitions and settings rules can have empty TOGETHER fields because their actual parent use is explicit and no separate mechanism is invented. The one general other-process gate is justified below; it is a source-stated prerequisite whose additional owner is not supplied. Success/failure interpretation is not an execution permission; unknown is not a retry class. Shared B8 operation/log/lifecycle contracts retain their canonical owners. Every internal edge, reverse use and cross-piece continuation is checked; one row names one place. The single BUILT input names only the already built preserved root store. No new result mechanism is stamped BUILT.

| Card | Plain gate justification |
|---|---|
| C-7O.6.4 — Other explicitly designed result-triggered process | V10 permits only another explicitly designed process; the source does not supply a further owner or trigger. This is the real prerequisite, not a fabricated component gate. |
